PID regional buses · Příbramsko (okres Příbram) · Monday 2 Nov 2026

PID Příbramsko

Every PID bus line of okres Příbram (licences 30xxxx) and the Prague and other lines whose outer end lies here, set as Simutrans lines with the owner's regional method: each line read over the whole day at its core points, a repeating interval, destination and shift variants on their own longer intervals (at most 480 min), timetables at the terminals worked back from the knots, every afternoon stop on a line, buses sized from the pattern. PID Příbramsko (okres Příbram with Dobříš and Sedlčany: licences 300xxx and the MHD Příbram lines 305xxx), the Prague lines whose outer end is in the okres (314, 317, 319, 360, 361, 392, 393, 395, 420) and the Jihočeský and Plzeňský long lines placed here (407, 416, 428, 438, 451, 454, 472, 477, 523). Timetable: PID GTFS of 1 Nov 2026, service day Mon 2 Nov 2026 (school day). Every terminus is treated as a loop (typed = real − 61 mod every). Most district lines are school-day lines of single trips; they are bent onto one real afternoon round each (every 120–480, 1 bus), kept where they are a place's only service, including the far ends in Jihočeský and Plzeňský kraj (owner, 2 Nov 2026: serve every place). Through-running: 393 buses go back to Prague as 395 trips at Příbram, Drkolnov (modelled, Simutrans line 393; the morning plates of 2 Nov already show every 393 trip on a 395 course) and on as 521 and 472 at Jiráskovy sady; the 450 bus runs the 549 Petrovice – Kuní trip and the 559 bus the 513 Vrchotice trips in the game (both folded into those lines). Checked on the 2 Nov live map: 500 trips go on as 510 at Jablonná, Horní Hbity (7221, 7223); 551 goes on as 552 at Kamýk (7501); 567 goes on as 472 at Třebsko on the 13:35 only (9832; the 15:30's bus comes back as the 17:11); 522 buses do not go on as 524 at Hvožďany (9612 and 9832 are not seen again until 20:00; the 524 16:45 is the 524's own bus 9567). From the timetable: 754 meets 486 at Osečany in the morning; 314 and 748 meet at Bratřínov (2 of 6). Timed connections, two operators: 517 → 317 at Dobříš, náměstí (most 517 arrivals). MHD Příbram rotates its buses between lines: 508 trips ending at Šachetní go on as 501 (4 of 4) and 503 departures from Svatá Hora follow 502 arrivals (13 of 14). That is why the town network's 18 buses here are more than the operator runs; pooling the town lines is the owner's option (not applied), and the 2 Nov live map will show the rotation. 317 (ČSAD Benešov) may later be pooled with 318 by the Prague bus sheet. The stops of okres Praha-západ (Davle, Hvozdnice and Bojanovice on 314, Mníšek pod Brdy on 317 and 319) and okres Benešov (754 Neveklov – Benešov, 519 Votice, 556 Heřmaničky, 454 Střezimíř) on these lines are served by no other PID line.

How to read the line cards

Typing the timetable. Values go into the Scheduling tab of the terminal entry, with the calendar on: Departure every, Offset, Also at (at most 7). Offsets count from 01:00 and every terminal is set as a loop, so each value is (real departure − 61) mod interval; the real clock minute is under every value. A row with Every 0 has no timetable: the bus leaves on arrival.

Core points and knots. Each line was read over the whole operating day (5:00–23:00) at its core points: terminals, knots where variants split, shared hubs. A trip counts at the time it passes a core point, wherever it starts. Where a slot is defined at a knot, the terminal departure is the knot time minus the running time, and the knot gets no timetable.

Variants. Each destination, path or shift variant is its own Simutrans line on a longer interval (the save game's Ex4). Shift lines repeat every 480 (8 h), the longest interval allowed. Trips more than 3 min off every slot are off the pattern, in orange.

Buses needed. One bus followed round the line's own slots with the real running times and at least 3 min at each terminal. Real buses swap lines at the terminals, so per-line totals are higher than what the operators run; pooling lines is the owner's choice and is not done.

In the game. Each card compares the save's line (export of 30 Oct 2026) with the modelled stops. The save's buses are shown as a check; the sheet is built from the real timetable and the buses seen running, so a game line may need buses added or moved.

To check

Choices made here that you may want to flip, each with its alternative; then the Simutrans lines whose model window has a slot without a real departure, or a real departure more than 3 min off.

Regional PID buses use the VZ-PID-bus objects, town lines the VZ-MHD<Town> ones (pak rules); objects are read from the installed pak. "requested" marks a type with no object yet (pak_request.md).

How this was built

Scope. PID GTFS downloaded Sun 1 Nov 2026 (feed 1-14 Nov). Service day Mon 2 Nov 2026 (the first live-map poll day), a normal school weekday (28 Oct was a holiday and 29-30 Oct autumn school holidays; regional lines run many school-day-only trips). The Středočeský kraj licenses each district's lines with its own prefix (30xxxx = okres Příbram); a line licensed by Prague or by another kraj is on the sheet of the okres of its outer end (the first stop, coming in from the far end, in a Středočeský municipality), or, with no Prague end, of the okres most of its stops lie in. 61 lines: 58 modelled here, 0 on the Prague bus sheet, 3 listed with the reason.

Method. pipeline/bus-schedule-method.md (v4): whole-day reading at core points, repeating interval, Ex4 variants, timing at terminals worked back from the knots, no daily lines (at most every 480), every afternoon stop on a line, buses from the pattern, every line checked by hand (method-check file in the pipeline folder).

Buses. PID live map (mapa.pid.cz getData.php), polled every minute 12:00-20:00 (every 3 min 5:00-12:00 and 20:00-23:00) on Mon 2 Nov 2026, and on Tue 3 Nov 2026 so far only 5:00-7:20 (this version, 3 Nov morning: the Tuesday trips fill only designed buses and missing types; the evening version adds the whole Tuesday), by scripts/01_live_poll.py; each bus once per line through getVehicleWindow.php (operator, type, course). A bus fills one Simutrans line it ran in that line's window, by matching, across all twelve oblast sheets; buses on the Prague bus sheet are not used here.

Sources: PID GTFS, PID live map, the save-game export of 30 Oct 2026 (engine session).
